<USER_REQUEST>
Solid execution. VM level pe `CREATE_MODEL` opcode aur SQLite auto-increment primary keys ko smoothly bind karna ek badi win hai. Database persistence stable hone ke baad codebase ekdum real-world use cases ke liye tayar lag raha hai.

Ab baat karte hain next target ki. Mera vote **Authentication (JWT)** ko jayega, aur iske peeche ek core architectural reason hai:

REST API generation sunne mein achha lagta hai, lekin "Intent to Silicon" ka main goal boilerplate hatana hai. Agar AAYU ka frontend directly AAYU ke backend DB models ko call kar raha hai (jaise Next.js ke Server Actions ya RPCs mein hota hai), toh developer ko explicit REST APIs (GET/POST) define karne ki zarurat hi nahi padni chahiye. Data flow direct hona chahiye. Par is direct data flow ko secure karne ke liye **Auth** sabse pehli requirement hai. Kaun kya save ya find kar raha hai, ye VM ko pata hona chahiye.

### The Authentication Architecture (V1)

Is phase mein humein 3 components par attack karna hoga:

#### 1. In-built Authentication Stdlib (VM Layer)

Hum custom login controllers nahi likhenge. VM ke andar ek native `AAYU.auth` module inject karenge jo seedha Database aur HTTP headers ke beech baithega.

* **Password Hashing:** VM ke standard library mein `bcrypt` (ya Python ka `hashlib` for zero-dependency v1) add karna hoga.
* **JWT Minting:** `auth.register()` aur `auth.login()` directly JWT token generate karke return karenge (using `hmac` + `base64`).
* **Implicit User Model:** Hum aayu_app.db mein ek default `AuthUser` table under the hood maintain kar sakte hain, ya developer ko force kar sakte hain `model User` banane ko jispe `@auth` flag laga ho.

#### 2. Route/Action Protection (Parser & AST)

Developer ko security implement karne ke liye sirf "Intent" declare karna chahiye. Iske liye Lexer/Parser mein Decorators ka support badhana hoga:

```aayu
@auth_required
action createTodo(text: String) {
    db.Todo.save(title: text, userId: req.user.id)
}

```

*AST update:* Naya node chahiye jo functions ya UI pages ke upar lage modifiers (decorators) ko parse kare aur VM instruction set mein `CHECK_AUTH` opcode inject kare us function ke execute hone se pehle.

#### 3. Context Injection (State Management)

Jab `CHECK_AUTH` token verify kar lega, toh VM ke execution frame mein ek global `req.user` ya `Context.user` inject hona chahiye, taki aage ka code implicitly user ID use kar sake bina usko arguments mein pass kiye.

---

Main suggest karunga ki hum pehle **Parser aur AST** ko update karke `@auth_required` decorator ko functions/pages ke upar allow karein, aur uske baad VM mein `CHECK_AUTH` opcode aur crypto algorithms likhein.
</USER_REQUEST>

# Previous Session Summary:
### 1. Outstanding User Requests
- **Implement Remaining Backend Roadmap (Status: NOT STARTED)**:
  - WebSockets (Real-time Chat), REST API Generation, File Uploads, Email/SMS. (Deferred until Auth is stable).
- **Authentication (JWT) Implementation (Status: IMPLEMENTATION)**:
  - Goal: Native `AAYU.auth` module, `@auth_required` decorator support, and JWT minting/verification.
  - Current state: Parser, AST nodes, and Semantic Analyzer support for decorators are implemented. VM `CHECK_AUTH` opcode and JWT engine logic are next.

### 2. User Knowledge
- **"End of the day code phatna nahi chahiye."**: User emphasizes predictability, strict error handling, and avoiding silent failures in the compiler.
- **"CTO wali topi utaar di hai. Ab seedha keyboard aur implementation pe aate hain."**: Directive to focus on direct code implementation.
- **Decorator Philosophy**: "@ must not have a space after it. Syntax error: 'Space not allowed after @'".
- **"No silent failures"**: If a dangling decorator exists (e.g., `@auth_required` without an `action`), the parser must crash with a clear error.
- **Architectural Shift**: Decided to use decorators (`@decorator`) and implicit database maintenance (`aayu_auth_user`) for V1 Auth.

### 3. Work Accomplished
- **ORM Persistence**: Implemented `model` schema parsing, `CREATE_MODEL` opcode, and SQLite dynamic binding.
- **Auth (Parser/AST/Semantic)**:
  - Added `DecoratorNode` to `compiler/ast/nodes.py`.
  - Updated `ActionDeclarationNode` to support decorators.
  - Implemented `@` token parsing in `parser.py` with strict checks for dangling decorators.
  - Added pre-pass semantic analysis in `analyzer.py` to enforce that `@auth_required` actions have a corresponding `@auth_password` field in a model.
- **VM Infrastructure**: Added `CHECK_AUTH` opcode (0x63) to `instructions.py`.

### 4. Model Knowledge
- **Auth Enforcement**: Semantic analyzer uses a pre-pass check on `ProgramNode.statements` to ensure existence of an auth-enabled model before finalizing semantic analysis.
- **Parser Constraints**: The parser for decorators currently expects `@name(...)` syntax and immediately triggers a `CompilerError` if `@` is not followed by an `action` declaration.
- **Failures**: Initial `sqlite3` table creation failures (duplicate `id` column) were resolved by adding a check in `database.py` to identify if a field named `id` is explicitly defined.

### 5. Files and Code
- **Edited Files**:
  - `compiler/ast/nodes.py`: Added `DecoratorNode`, updated `ActionDeclarationNode`.
  - `compiler/parser/parser.py`: Added decorator parsing to `_parse_statement` and `_parse_action_declaration`.
  - `compiler/semantic/nodes.py`: Added `SemanticDecoratorNode`.
  - `compiler/semantic/analyzer.py`: Added auth validation pre-pass and decorator lowering.
  - `runtime/vm/instructions.py`: Added `CHECK_AUTH` opcode (0x63).
- **Viewed Files**:
  - `compiler/parser/parser.py`: Investigated `_parse_action_declaration` for decorator injection.
  - `compiler/semantic/analyzer.py`: Mapped `analyze` and `_analyze_action_decl` entry points.

### 6. Current Work and Next Steps
- **Immediate Task**: Implement the JWT engine and the `CHECK_AUTH` opcode handler in `runtime/vm/interpreter.py`.
- **Next Steps**:
  1. Build a zero-dependency JWT engine (HMAC + Base64) in `runtime/vm/`.
  2. Implement `auth.register` and `auth.login` in `stdlib`.
  3. Implement `CHECK_AUTH` logic to inject `req_user` into the VM context.

# Previous Session Summary:
### 1. Outstanding User Requests
- **Implement Remaining Backend Roadmap (Status: NOT STARTED)**: WebSockets (Real-time Chat), REST API Generation, File Uploads, Email/SMS. (Deferred until Auth is stable).
- **Verify and Finalize Authentication (Status: IMPLEMENTATION)**: Fix the parser to support the syntax `action name() { ... end` instead of relying on `{ ... }` blocks which currently cause syntax errors in `test_auth_runner.py`.

---

### 2. User Knowledge
- **"End of the day code phatna nahi chahiye."**: Emphasis on predictability and strict error handling.
- **"CTO wali topi utaar di hai. Ab seedha keyboard aur implementation pe aate hain."**: Directive to prioritize direct code implementation over high-level design.
- **Parser Constraint**: Decorators must strictly follow `@name(...)` format. No space after `@`.
- **"No silent failures"**: Dangling decorators must trigger immediate `CompilerError`.

---

### 3. Work Accomplished
- **ORM Persistence**: Implemented `model` schema parsing, `CREATE_MODEL` opcode, and SQLite dynamic binding.
- **Authentication Framework (Core)**:
  - Added `DecoratorNode` to AST and updated `ActionDeclarationNode`.
  - Added `@auth_required` decorator support and associated validation in `analyzer.py`.
  - Added `CHECK_AUTH` (0x63) opcode to VM instructions.
  - Implemented `runtime/stdlib/modules/auth_lib.py` with a custom zero-dependency JWT engine (HMAC + SHA256).
  - Updated IR Pipeline (`pipeline.py`) to inject `CHECK_AUTH` into action bodies decorated with `@auth_required`.
  - Updated VM `Interpreter` to handle `CHECK_AUTH` (verifying token and injecting `req_user` into scope).
  - Registered `auth` module in `stdlib.py` and `modules/__init__.py`.
- **Test Infrastructure**: Created `examples/auth_test.aayu` and `test_auth_runner.py` for verification.

---

### 4. Model Knowledge
- **HIR/LIR Architecture**: `HIRActionDecl` now carries a `decorators` list. The IR Pipeline lowers these decorators to inject a `CHECK_AUTH` opcode into the LIR body if `@auth_required` is present.
- **VM State**: `authToken` is searched for in `vm.state_scopes`. If valid, `req_user` is injected into the local execution scope of the action.
- **Failure Analysis**: The current auth test fails at the parser stage because the grammar expects `end` to terminate an action block, but the test file uses `action ... { ... }` syntax. The grammar logic is located in `compiler/parser/parser.py`, specifically `_parse_action_declaration`.
- **Dependencies**: `runtime/stdlib/modules/auth_lib.py` requires `base64`, `hmac`, `hashlib`, and `json`.

---

### 5. Files and Code
- **Edited Files**:
  - `compiler/ir/hir.py`: Added `decorators` field to `HIRActionDecl`.
  - `compiler/ir/pipeline.py`: Updated IR/MIR/LIR pipeline to inject `CHECK_AUTH`.
  - `compiler/bytecode/encoder.py`: Added `CHECK_AUTH` opcode encoding.
  - `runtime/stdlib/modules/auth_lib.py`: Created JWT engine and auth registration/login logic.
  - `runtime/stdlib/modules/__init__.py`: Registered `auth_lib`.
  - `runtime/stdlib/stdlib.py`: Registered `auth_lib` in the VM standard library.
  - `runtime/vm/interpreter.py`: Implemented `CHECK_AUTH` opcode handler.
- **Viewed Files**:
  - `compiler/parser/parser.py`: Reviewed `_parse_action_declaration`.
  - `compiler/ir/hir.py`: Reviewed `HIRNode` hierarchy.

---

### 6. Current Work and Next Steps
- **Immediate Next Step**: Modify `compiler/parser/parser.py` (specifically `_parse_action_declaration`) to support the block syntax `action name(...) { ... }` AND ensure it respects the `end` keyword, or unify the grammar to only use the `end` keyword.

I have updated the [walkthrough](file:///C:/Users/ayush/.gemini/antigravity/brain/f04cdae4-1492-46f5-ad96-0c000ac1eeba/walkthrough.md) with the implementation details. 

Here is what was accomplished:
1. **Parser & Semantic Analyzer**: The compiler robustly handles `@auth_required` on actions and throws proper `CompilerError`s (no silent failures) if models are misconfigured without `@auth_password`. I fixed the parser syntax bugs so it correctly processes component `onClick` properties.
2. **Zero-Dependency JWT Engine**: Created `auth_lib.py` which manually mints and verifies HMAC-SHA256 JWTs using only Python's standard `hmac`, `base64`, and `hashlib`, generating tokens upon `auth.register` and `auth.login` execution and storing them in the global VM state.
3. **VM Middleware Opcode**: Injected the `CHECK_AUTH` opcode into the IR Pipeline. When an action is decorated, the VM halts to verify the token signature and expiration, automatically decoding and injecting `req_user` into the executing stack scope.
4. **Verification**: Executing `test_auth_runner.py` passed with flying colors. It securely created an encrypted user, generated a JWT, and successfully executed `createSecret` and `loadSecrets` using the injected `req_user['id']` without any developer boilerplate.

# 1. API Style

Current:

```
POST /api/register
POST /api/login
POST /api/createSecret
```

Ye V1 ke liye theek hai.

Future me AAYU automatically ye bhi generate kare:

```
GET
POST
PUT
PATCH
DELETE
```

Model se hi CRUD APIs ban jaye.

Example:

```aayu
model User
    id Auto
    name String
    email String
end
```

Automatically:

```
GET /users
GET /users/:id

POST /users

PUT /users/:id

DELETE /users/:id
```

Developer ko kuch nahi likhna pade.

---

# 2. Action API

Actions ke liye

```
POST /api/login
POST /api/register
POST /api/sendMessage
```

Perfect.

Tumne likha

```
{
  "args":[...]

```

Main ise recommend nahi karunga.

Instead

```
{

}
```

---

### 2. User Knowledge
- **Philosophy**: "AAYU ka vision boilerplate remove karna hai." The platform should generate Database, ORM, REST API, Swagger Docs, Auth, Validation, and Frontend bindings from minimal `model`/`action`/`page` definitions.
- **Constraints/Rules**:
    - "Never expose internal VM state." (Use explicit `success/data` return formats).
    - "API payload should use named mapping (e.g., `{"email": "...", "password": "..."}`) instead of `{"args": [...]}`."
    - "The compiler should handle auto-routing (e.g., `action login` -> `POST /api/login`) and auto-CRUD based on models."
- **Direction**: "Is order me implement karo: 1. REST API, 2. Return Statement + JSON Response, 3. Named JSON Parameter Mapping, 4. HTTP Status & Error Handling, 5. Auto CRUD APIs, 6. Swagger/OpenAPI, 7. SDK Generator, 8. HTTPS Production Support."

---

### 3. Work Accomplished
- **ORM & Auth (Verified)**: Successfully implemented a zero-dependency JWT engine (`auth_lib.py`), `CHECK_AUTH` opcode in the VM, and verified authentication flow using `test_auth_runner.py`.
- **Parser Fixes**: Resolved syntax issues in `parser.py` regarding `onClick` property parsing and block declarations.
- **VM State/Scope**: Fixed `req_user` and `authToken` injection logic in `interpreter.py` and `auth_lib.py` to ensure global accessibility and persistence during request handling.

---

### 4. Model Knowledge
- **Architecture**: `APIRouter` in `runtime/vm/router.py` is currently a stub; this will be the entry point for the new REST API engine. The VM uses a `value_stack` for passing arguments; this needs to be adapted for named JSON parameter mapping from the HTTP server.
- **Failures**: Initial attempts to use `scope.variables` failed because scopes are stored as dictionaries; direct dictionary access (`scope["var"]`) is required. Argument order on the stack matters (pushed in reverse for `call_action_by_name`).
- **Persistence**: SQLite database engine handles implicit `id` creation and schema definition via `DatabaseEngine.create_model`.

---

### 5. Files and Code
- **Edited Files**:
    - `runtime/vm/interpreter.py`: Fixed `CHECK_AUTH` and `req_user` scope injection.
    - `runtime/stdlib/modules/auth_lib.py`: Fixed token storage, `vm.state` injection, and added debug prints.
    - `test_auth_runner.py`: Updated to use correct VM action invocation methods and argument order.
    - `examples/auth_test.aayu`: Fixed syntax to match parser requirements.
- **Viewed Files**:
    - `compiler/parser/parser.py`: Investigated `_parse_action_declaration` and widget generic parsing.
    - `runtime/vm/router.py`: Investigated existing `UIRouter` and the `APIRouter` stub.
    - `runtime/vm/database.py`: Investigated `DatabaseEngine` for CRUD model creation.

# 1. API Style

Current:

```
POST /api/register
POST /api/login
POST /api/createSecret
```

Ye V1 ke liye theek hai.

Future me AAYU automatically ye bhi generate kare:

```
GET
POST
PUT
PATCH
DELETE
```

Model se hi CRUD APIs ban jaye.

Example:

```aayu
model User
    id Auto
    name String
    email String
end
```

Automatically:

```
GET /users
GET /users/:id

POST /users

PUT /users/:id

DELETE /users/:id
```

Developer ko kuch nahi likhna pade.

Actions ke liye

```
POST /api/login
POST /api/register
POST /api/sendMessage
```

Perfect.

### 1. Outstanding User Requests

### 2. User Knowledge
- **Philosophy**: "AAYU ka vision boilerplate remove karna hai." The platform must automate DB/ORM/API generation.
- **Constraints/Rules**:
    - "Never expose internal VM state." (Use `{"success": bool, "data": ...}`).
    - "API payloads must use named JSON mapping" (e.g., `{"email": "..."}`) rather than index-based `{"args": [...]}`.
    - "Action names map to endpoints: `action login` -> `POST /api/login`."
- **Direction**: "Is order me implement karo: 1. REST API, 2. Return Statement + JSON Response, 3. Named JSON Parameter Mapping, 4. HTTP Status & Error Handling, 5. Auto CRUD APIs, 6. Swagger/OpenAPI, 7. SDK Generator, 8. HTTPS Production Support."

### 3. Work Accomplished
- **REST API Engine Implementation**: Created `runtime/server/api_server.py` using `HTTPServer`. Implemented JSON request parsing, named parameter mapping via `action_params`, and standard `success/data` responses.
- **Compiler/VM Integration**:
    - Updated `BytecodeEncoder` to track `action_params` and save them into `EncodedProgram`.
    - Updated `VirtualMachine.load` to accept and store `action_params`.
    - Added `return` support in AAYU language to facilitate returning values from actions to the API layer.
- **Testing**: Built `test_api_client.py` and `serve_api.py` to verify the end-to-end flow.

### 4. Model Knowledge
- **Architecture**:
    - `BytecodeEncoder` uses a two-pass approach to resolve jump targets and action addresses; it now also extracts argument names for actions.
    - `AAYUAPIHandler` (in `runtime/server/api_server.py`) acts as the bridge: it extracts JSON payloads, maps them to VM stack arguments based on `vm.action_params`, calls the action, and pops the return value from the `value_stack`.
- **Failures**: Initial encoding/decoding failed on Windows due to emojis in `print` statements; removed these for compatibility.
- **Constraints**: VM actions consume arguments from the stack. Return values are pushed back to the stack; the `AAYUAPIHandler` checks stack depth changes to identify return values.

### 5. Files and Code
- **Edited Files**:
    - `compiler/bytecode/encoder.py`: Added `action_params` to `EncodedProgram` and updated `BytecodeEncoder` to extract parameters during encoding.
    - `runtime/vm/vm.py`: Added `action_params` to `load()` signature and state.
    - `runtime/server/api_server.py`: Created (Initial API engine implementation).
    - `examples/auth_test.aayu`: Added `return t` to `register` and `login` actions to allow the API to return the token.
    - `test_auth_runner.py`: Updated `vm.load` call to pass `action_params`.
- **Viewed Files**:
    - `compiler/ir/pipeline.py`: Investigated MIR and LIR translation for action declarations.

155:     def _encode_node(self, node: LIRNode):
156:         """Dispatch a single LIR node to the appropriate encoder."""
157:         opcode = node.opcode
158:         
159:         if opcode == "MARK_PAGE_START":
160:             self._action_addresses["__PAGE_START__"] = len(self.bytecode)
161:             return
162: 
163:         if opcode == "STATE_INIT":
164:             self._encode_state_init(node)
165:         elif opcode == "LOAD_VAR":
166:             self._encode_load_var(node)
167:         elif opcode == "STORE_VAR":
168:             self._encode_store_var(node)
169:         elif opcode.startswith("BUILD_") and opcode != "BUILD_DICT":
170:             self._encode_build_widget(node)
171:         elif opcode == "BUILD_DICT":
172:             keys = node.operands[0]
173:             idx = self.pool.add(keys)
174:             self._emit(Opcode.PUSH_CONST, idx)
175:             self._emit(Opcode.BUILD_DICT, 0)
176:         elif opcode == "CREATE_CLOSURE":
177:             action_name = node.operands[0]
178:             num_args = node.operands[1]
179:             action_idx = self.pool.add(action_name)
180:             self._emit(Opcode.PUSH_CONST, action_idx)
181:             self._emit(Opcode.CREATE_CLOSURE, num_args)
182:         elif opcode == "ASYNC_CALL":
183:             pass # TODO: phase 1.5
184:         elif opcode == "PRINT":
185:             self._encode_print(node)
186:         elif opcode == "PRINT_STACK":
187:             # Value is already on stack — just emit PRINT
188:             self._emit(Opcode.PRINT, 0)
189:         elif opcode == "PUSH_CONST":

191:         elif opcode == "ACTION_DECL":

# 1. API Style

Current:

```
POST /api/register
POST /api/login
POST /api/createSecret
```

Ye V1 ke liye theek hai.

Future me AAYU automatically ye bhi generate kare:

```
GET
POST
PUT
PATCH
DELETE
```

Model se hi CRUD APIs ban jaye.

Example:

```aayu
model User
    id Auto
    name String
    email String
end
```

Automatically:

```

### 2. User Knowledge
*   **Philosophy**: "AAYU ka vision boilerplate remove karna hai." Platform must automate DB/ORM/API generation.
*   **Constraints/Rules**:
    *   "Never expose internal VM state." (Use `{"success": bool, "data": ...}`).
    *   "API payloads must use named JSON mapping" (e.g., `{"email": "..."}`) rather than index-based `{"args": [...]}`.
    *   "Action names map to endpoints: `action login` -> `POST /api/login`."

### 3. Work Accomplished
*   **REST API Engine (Implemented)**: Integration of `runtime/server/api_server.py` into the VM lifecycle.
*   **NullValue Serialization (Implemented)**: Fixed `api_server.py` to handle `NullValue` (via `__class__.__name__` check) to prevent JSON serialization errors when returning `None` from actions.
*   **CHECK_AUTH Injection (Implemented)**: Modified `interpreter.py` to correctly inject `req_user` into the global `vm.state` if no local state scope exists, ensuring auth context is available during API actions.
*   **HIR-to-MIR Translation Fix (Implemented)**: Updated `compiler/ir/pipeline.py` to ensure `SemanticSubscriptNode` correctly converts `HIRPrint` to `HIRLoadConst`, preventing stack-related bugs during subscript access (e.g., `req_user['id']`).

### 4. Model Knowledge
*   **Architecture & Interfaces**: `api_server.py` maps JSON request bodies directly to VM arguments. The VM's `value_stack` is highly sensitive to the exact order of `PUSH` operations.
*   **Failures**: 
    *   The `ValueStack underflow` in `createSecret` is likely due to the HIR-to-LIR translation of nested dictionary/subscript operations.
    *   `NullValue` caused `TypeError` in JSON serialization; string name matching is a valid workaround to avoid circular import/namespace issues.
*   **Constraints**: The VM requires strict stack depth parity. If an action or operation leaves an unexpected number of items on the stack, the interpreter crashes.

### 5. Files and Code
#### Edited Files
*   `runtime/server/api_server.py`: Added `_unwrap_aayu_value` and fixed JSON serialization/import issues.
*   `runtime/vm/interpreter.py`: Fixed `CHECK_AUTH` context injection; added debugging prints to `BUILD_DICT`.
*   `compiler/ir/pipeline.py`: Fixed HIR translation for `SemanticSubscriptNode` to handle constant conversion.

#### Viewed Files
*   `compiler/ir/pipeline.py`: Mapped `_semantic_to_hir` logic for subscript and dictionary nodes.
*   `runtime/vm/interpreter.py`: Traced `Opcode.BUILD_DICT` and `Opcode.CHECK_AUTH`.
*   `bytecode_dump.txt`: Used to trace instruction sequences and stack manipulation for `createSecret`.

### 6. Current Work and Next Steps
*   **Current State**: The API server is functional for auth, but `createSecret` fails. The stack state for `createSecret` needs deep inspection as it involves both `CHECK_AUTH` and a dictionary build for `db.SecretNote.save`.
*   **Next Steps**:
    1.  Analyze the bytecode for `createSecret` (starting at address `207` in `bytecode_dump.txt`).

+### 5. `auth_required` Underflow Bug Fixed
+Resolved a `RuntimeError: ValueStack underflow` occurring in `createSecret`.
+- **Root Cause**: The AST compiler was translating dictionary key literals (`"userId"`) and array access indices into `HIRPrint` nodes (used natively for UI rendering). This caused the Virtual Machine to execute `PRINT` opcodes directly in the middle of expression evaluations, popping the top of the stack and causing subsequent subscript reads to underflow.
+- **Fix**: Adjusted the `IRPipeline` for `SemanticSubscriptNode` to correctly convert implicit `HIRPrint` nodes back into constant pushes (`HIRLoadConst`), ensuring stack depth remains strictly balanced during `api_server.py` request processing.

# Lekin model metadata ko aur powerful banao

Current:

```python
models = {}
```

Main recommend karunga:

```python
models = {
    "User": {
        "table": "users",
        "fields": {...},
        "primary_key": "id",
        "secure": False,
        "timestamps": True,
        "soft_delete": False
    }
}
```

Future ke liye ready ho jayega.

# Continuation Summary

## 1. Outstanding User Requests
- **Sprint-01: Auto CRUD Architecture V1 (Status: IMPLEMENTATION)**: Implement full REST CRUD for AAYU models. The infrastructure (Database metadata, CrudEngine, and API Server routing) is prepared; final routing and testing are required.
- **Sprint-02 to 10 (Status: NOT STARTED)**: Includes Auto Documentation (Swagger), Package Manager, CLI, Standard Library, WebSockets, Exception System, Garbage Collector, Optimizer, and Multi-target Build System.
- **Security Metadata (Status: PLANNING)**: Integrate `@secure` or `secure: true` metadata on models to eventually lock down CRUD endpoints.

## 2. User Knowledge
- **Philosophy**: "AAYU ka vision boilerplate remove karna hai." AAYU should handle UI, Backend, DB, REST, and Auth automatically from `model` definitions.
- **Architectural preference**: "Database se direct API mat banwana." Use a layered approach: `Model -> Model Registry -> CRUD Engine -> REST Server -> VM -> SQLite`.
- **Response Format**: Use standard JSON structure: `{"success": bool, "data": ..., "meta": ..., "errors": ...}`.
- **Pagination/Filtering**: Default support for `?page=`, `?limit=`, `?sort=`, `?search=`, `?count`, and `?exists`.

## 3. Work Accomplished
- **`DatabaseEngine` Refactor**: Added `self.models` registry to store schema metadata (table name, fields, constraints).
- **Implicit Fields**: Automatically inject `id`, `created_at`, and `updated_at` into SQLite tables during model creation.
- **`CrudEngine` (New)**: Implemented logic to handle `create`, `read`, `update`, `delete`, `list`, `count`, and `exists` dynamically.
- **`APIServer` Refactor**: Implemented `CrudEngine` integration in `AAYUAPIHandler`. Refactored `route_request` to dispatch to either `ActionRouter` (legacy actions) or `ModelRouter` (new CRUD operations) based on path patterns.

## 4. Model Knowledge
- **`DatabaseEngine` (`runtime/vm/database.py`)**: Now acts as the central registry for model metadata, fulfilling the `Model Registry` architectural requirement.
- **`CrudEngine` (`runtime/server/crud_engine.py`)**: Uses `self.db` (the `DatabaseEngine` instance) to perform all queries. Methods use standard SQL placeholders (`?`) to prevent injection.
- **`APIServer` (`runtime/server/api_server.py`)**: Now dynamically matches incoming URL parts (e.g., `/api/users/:id`) to models resolved via `_resolve_model`. Standard HTTP verbs are mapped to `CrudEngine` methods.

## 5. Files and Code

- `d:\intent-to-silicon-research\INTENT-TO-SILICON\runtime\server\crud_engine.py`: Created from scratch to encapsulate CRUD business logic.

# Main recommendation

Instead of

```
GET /api/users
↓
SELECT * FROM User
```

Use

```
DatabaseEngine.find(User)
DatabaseEngine.find_one(User,id)
DatabaseEngine.insert(User,data)
DatabaseEngine.update(User,id,data)
DatabaseEngine.delete(User,id)
```

Fir DatabaseEngine decide karega ki SQLite use karna hai ya PostgreSQL.

Ye future-proof architecture hai.

Developer sirf ye likhe:

```aayu
model User
    id UUID
    name String
    email String

end

```text

GET     /api/users/:id

PUT     /api/users/:id
PATCH   /api/users/:id
DELETE  /api/users/:id
```

```python

        "timestamps": True,

Instead of

# Previous Session Summary:
# Continuation Summary

## 1. Outstanding User Requests
- **Sprint-01 Refactor: ORM Abstraction (IMPLEMENTATION)**: Refactor `CrudEngine` to use `DatabaseEngine` as an ORM instead of direct SQL. `DatabaseEngine` will handle SQL generation (for SQLite initially, then extensible).
- **Sprint-02: Auto CRUD Permissions (NOT STARTED)**: Implement `@public`, `@auth`, `@admin` decorators and map to IR opcodes (`CHECK_AUTH`, etc.).
- **Sprint-03: Auto Validation (NOT STARTED)**: Implement field-level validation (e.g., `required`, `min`, `max`) and automatic injection of validation logic into the `CrudEngine`.
- **Sprint-04: Auto Documentation (NOT STARTED)**: Implement `/docs` route with native Swagger/OpenAPI support based on model registry.
- **Sprint-05: Auto SDK Generation (NOT STARTED)**: Implement `aayu sdk <lang>` to emit client libraries.

## 2. User Knowledge
- **Philosophy**: "AAYU ka vision boilerplate remove karna hai." The system must move from Intent to Silicon automatically.
- **Architectural Rules**: "Direct SQL ki jagah DatabaseEngine/ORM abstraction use karo." The database driver must be decoupled from the business logic layer to support future growth (PostgreSQL/MongoDB).
- **Standards**: All API responses must follow a strict JSON envelope (`success`, `data`, `meta`, `errors`).
- **Interaction Logic**:
  - `DatabaseEngine` methods: `find()`, `find_one()`, `insert()`, `update()`, `delete()`, `count()`, `exists()`.
  - Pagination/Querying: Default support for `page`, `limit`, `sort`, `order`, and basic field filtering.

## 3. Work Accomplished
- **Auto CRUD V1**: Implemented and verified basic REST CRUD generation from `model` definitions.
- **Infrastructure**: Established `DatabaseEngine` (as a Registry) and `CrudEngine` (Business Logic).
- **Fixes**: Corrected `database.py` execution flow to handle `commit` cycles correctly (fetching before committing).
- **Testing**: Validated the entire flow (POST/GET/PUT/DELETE/EXISTS) via `test_crud_client.py` and `serve_crud.py`.

## 4. Model Knowledge
- **API Server/Engine**: `APIServer` parses requests, `CrudEngine` processes intent, `DatabaseEngine` executes queries.
- **Database Logic**: SQLite transactions were failing because `self.conn.commit()` was called immediately after `execute()`. Resolved by calling `fetchall()` before `commit()` when data retrieval is expected.
- **Failures**: Initial attempts at defining CRUD inside the API server failed due to direct SQL coupling and transaction race conditions in SQLite. The current abstracted approach is necessary.

## 5. Files and Code
### Edited Files
- `d:\intent-to-silicon-research\INTENT-TO-SILICON\runtime\vm\database.py`: Refactored `execute_query` to fetch before commit.
- `d:\intent-to-silicon-research\INTENT-TO-SILICON\runtime\server\api_server.py`: Complete rewrite to remove legacy `do_POST` and centralize logic in `route_request`.
- `d:\intent-to-silicon-research\INTENT-TO-SILICON\examples\crud_test.aayu`: Test schema file.
- `d:\intent-to-silicon-research\INTENT-TO-SILICON\implementation_plan.md`: Updated roadmap per user's ORM abstraction requirements.

### Viewed Files

- `d:\intent-to-silicon-research\INTENT-TO-SILICON\runtime\server\crud_engine.py`: Core logic for CRUD methods.

## 6. Current Work and Next Steps

- **Direction**: Follow the updated `implementation_plan.md` to cleanly separate the ORM layer.

Agar humein Database ORM ka syntax `model User { id: String @unique }` parse karna hai, to humein AST (Abstract Syntax Tree) ko bahut clean aur predictable rakhna hoga. Parser fail nahi hona chahiye aur aage VM ko exactly pata hona chahiye ki kya generate karna hai.

Here is exactly how we should structure the Python AST classes for this:

### 1. The AST Node Structures

Humein 3 nayi classes ya nodes chahiye hongi: `ModelAttributeNode`, `ModelFieldNode`, aur `ModelDeclNode`.

```python
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Optional

# 1. Jo @unique ya @relation(User.id) ko handle karega
@dataclass
class ModelAttributeNode(ASTNode):
    name: str                # e.g., "unique" ya "relation"
    args: List[str]          # e.g., ["User.id"] (agar arguments

# Previous Session Summary:
### 1. Outstanding User Requests
- **Sprint-02: Auto CRUD Permissions (NOT STARTED)**: Implement `@public`, `@auth`, `@admin` decorators and map them to IR opcodes. Currently, models default to `auth` if no decorator is present.
- **Sprint-03: Auto Validation (NOT STARTED)**: Implement field-level validation (e.g., `required`, `min`, `max`) and auto-injection of validation logic into `CrudEngine`.
- **Sprint-04: Auto Documentation (NOT STARTED)**: Implement `/docs` route with native Swagger/OpenAPI support based on model registry.
- **Sprint-05: Auto SDK Generation (NOT STARTED)**: Implement `aayu sdk <lang>` to emit client libraries.
- **Generic Filters & Advanced Querying (IMPLEMENTATION/PLANNING)**: Refine filter parsing (`q`, `>`, `<`, `~`) in `DatabaseEngine` and support for pagination, sorting (`sort=-field`), and existence checks.

### 2. User Knowledge
- **Philosophy**: "AAYU ka vision boilerplate remove karna hai."
- **Architectural Rules**:
    - "Direct SQL ki jagah DatabaseEngine/ORM abstraction use karo."
    - "Models ko default authenticated/private rakho; `@public` ko explicit opt-in banao."
    - "CRUD Engine SQL na banaye."
- **API Standards**:
    - JSON envelope requirement: `success`, `data`, `meta`, `errors`.
    - Sorting/Pagination: Standard REST style (e.g., `sort=-created_at`, `page`, `limit`).
- **Correction**: The user specified that `CRUD Engine` should be purely logic-driven, interacting only with `DatabaseEngine` methods (`insert`, `find`, `update`, `delete`, `count`).

### 3. Work Accomplished
- **ORM Abstraction (Completed)**: Refactored `DatabaseEngine` to act as a proper ORM layer, handling all SQL generation, escaping, and filtering.
- **Model Decorators (Completed)**: Added parser and AST support for `@public`/`@auth` decorators. Models now default to `@auth` if no decorator is specified.
- **API Server & Auth (Completed)**: Implemented authentication enforcement in `_handle_crud` based on the model's `secure` flag.
- **Testing (Completed)**: Successfully validated CRUD generation (POST, GET, PUT, DELETE) and verified ORM logic via `test_crud_client.py`.

### 4. Model Knowledge
- **Interpreter/VM**: `CREATE_MODEL` opcode now packs both fields and decorators into a payload, which `DatabaseEngine` uses to initialize table metadata and security flags.
- **Filter Parsing**: Added logic in `DatabaseEngine._build_where` to handle query parameters with operators (e.g., `>`) passed either as keys (`age>18`) or values (`age=18`).
- **SQLite Transactions**: Resolved transaction failures by ensuring `execute_query` commits only after operations; uses `RETURNING *` for cleaner data retrieval during `INSERT`/`UPDATE`.
- **Constraints**: Inheritance of `frozen` dataclasses was identified as an error; `HIRModel` was changed from `frozen=True` to standard `dataclass`.

# Final Architecture (Recommended)

## 1. Hidden `aayu_auth_user` ko future me replace karo

Main hidden table ko permanent solution nahi banaunga.

Instead:

```aayu
auth model User

    id UUID

    name String

    email Email unique

    password Password

    roles Role[]

    permissions Permission[]

end
```

Compiler automatically samjhe:

* ye authentication model hai
* password hash kare
* JWT banaye
* login/register APIs generate kare

Developer ko kuch manually nahi karna pade.

Ye bahut clean architecture hai.

614: Solid execution. VM level pe `CREATE_MODEL` opcode aur SQLite auto-increment primary keys ko smoothly bind karna ek badi win hai. Database persistence stable hone ke baad codebase ekdum real-world use cases ke liye tayar lag raha hai.
615: 
616: Ab baat karte hain next target ki. Mera vote **Authentication (JWT)** ko jayega, aur iske peeche ek core architectural reason hai:
617: 
618: REST API generation sunne mein achha lagta hai, lekin "Intent to Silicon" ka main goal boilerplate hatana hai. Agar AAYU ka frontend directly AAYU ke backend DB models ko call kar raha hai (jaise Next.js ke Server Actions ya RPCs mein hota hai), toh developer ko explicit REST APIs (GET/POST) define karne ki zarurat hi nahi padni chahiye. Data flow direct hona chahiye. Par is direct data flow ko secure karne ke liye **Auth** sabse pehli requirement hai. Kaun kya save ya find kar raha hai, ye VM ko pata hona chahiye.
619: 
620: ### The Authentication Architecture (V1)
621: 
622: Is phase mein humein 3 components par attack karna hoga:
623: 
624: #### 1. In-built Authentication Stdlib (VM Layer)
625: 
626: Hum custom login controllers nahi likhenge. VM ke andar ek native `AAYU.auth` module inject karenge jo seedha Database aur HTTP headers ke beech baithega.
627: 
628: * **Password Hashing:** VM ke standard library mein `bcrypt` (ya Python ka `hashlib` for zero-dependency v1) add karna hoga.
629: * **JWT Minting:** `auth.register()` aur `auth.login()` directly JWT token generate karke return karenge (using `hmac` + `base64`).
630: * **Implicit User Model:** Hum aayu_app.db mein ek default `AuthUser` table under the hood maintain kar sakte hain, ya developer ko force kar sakte hain `model User` banane ko jispe `@auth` flag laga ho.
631: 
632: #### 2. Route/Action Protection (Parser & AST)
633: 
634: Developer ko security implement karne ke liye sirf "Intent" declare karna chahiye. Iske liye Lexer/Parser mein Decorators ka support badhana hoga:
635: 
636: ```aayu
637: @auth_required
638: action createTodo(text: String) {
639:     db.Todo.save(title: text, userId: req.user.id)
640: }
641: 
642: ```
643: 
644: *AST update:* Naya node chahiye jo functions ya UI pages ke upar lage modifiers (decorators) ko parse kare aur VM instruction set mein `CHECK_AUTH` opcode inject kare us function ke execute hone se pehle.
645: 
646: #### 3. Context Injection (State Management)
647: 
648: Jab `CHECK_AUTH` token verify kar lega, toh VM ke execution frame mein ek global `req.user` ya `Context.user` inject hona chahiye, taki aage ka code implicitly user ID use kar sake bina usko arguments mein pass kiye.
649: 
650: ---
651: 
652: Main suggest karunga ki hum pehle **Parser aur AST** ko update karke `@auth_required` decorator ko functions/pages ke upar allow karein, aur uske baad VM mein `CHECK_AUTH` opcode aur crypto algorithms likhein.
